<a id="cp-00001"></a>

## CP-00001｜路程、位移与位移的大小

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 运动学／路程与位移

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

举出一个实例，使物体运动的路程、位移以及位移的大小三者之间的区别清晰可见，并在实例中明确指出这三个量分别是什么。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Displacement — CP-00001

Give an example in which there are clear distinctions among distance traveled, displacement, and magnitude of displacement. Specifically identify each quantity in your example.

<a id="cp-00002"></a>

## CP-00002｜路程何时等于位移的大小

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 运动学／路程与位移

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

在什么情况下，物体走过的**路程**等于其**位移的大小**？位移的大小与位移本身在什么唯一情形下可以说完全相同？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Displacement — CP-00002

Under what circumstances does distance traveled equal magnitude of displacement? What is the only case in which magnitude of displacement and displacement are exactly the same?

## 来源

- 原件：external_sources/college_physics/raw/CP-00002.md
- 原件 Git blob SHA：81feaa8082ea2c0471f2cf1cb6cb85bf0fd555de

<a id="cp-00003"></a>

## CP-00003｜细菌来回游动时的路程与位移

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 运动学／路程与位移

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

细菌可利用鞭毛（类似细小尾巴的结构）往复游动，其速率可达 $50\,\mathrm{\mu m/s}$，即 $50\times10^{-6}\,\mathrm{m/s}$。相对于细菌自身尺寸而言，它游过的**总路程**可能很长，但**位移**却很小。为什么？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Displacement — CP-00003

Bacteria move back and forth by using their flagella (structures that look like little tails). Speeds of up to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext fontstyle="normal">50 μm/s </mtext><mspace width="0.25em" /><mfenced open="(" close=")"><mrow><mrow><mtext>50</mtext><mo stretchy="false">×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">−</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mrow></msup></mrow><mspace width="0.25em" /><mtext> m/s</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"50 μm/s " left ("50" times "10" rSup { size 8{ - 6} } " m/s" right )} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 have been observed. The total distance traveled by a bacterium is large for its size, while its displacement is small. Why is this?

<a id="cp-00004"></a>

## CP-00004｜水平地面上斜抛运动的速度分析

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／速度和速率

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

不计空气阻力，物体由水平地面以非 $0^\circ$、非 $90^\circ$ 的仰角抛出，并落回同一水平面。回答：（a）速度矢量有可能为零吗？（b）何时速度的大小最小？何时最大？（c）在 $t=0$ 以外的时刻，速度矢量能否与初速度完全相同？（d）在 $t=0$ 以外的时刻，速率能否与初速率相同？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00004

Answer the following questions for projectile motion on level ground assuming negligible air resistance (the initial angle being neither <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mtext>0º</mtext></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"0°"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 nor <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mtext>90º</mtext></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"90°"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

): (a) Is the velocity ever zero? (b) When is the velocity a minimum? A maximum? (c) Can the velocity ever be the same as the initial velocity at a time other than at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo stretchy="false">=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{t=0} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

? (d) Can the speed ever be the same as the initial speed at a time other than at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo stretchy="false">=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{t=0} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

?

## 来源

- 原件：external_sources/college_physics/raw/CP-00004.md
- 原件 Git blob SHA：1f735d176ad4193cce177bbf5fc42e15832e4569

<a id="cp-00005"></a>

## CP-00005｜斜抛运动中加速度与速度分量的关系

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／加速度分量

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

仍考虑从水平地面斜向抛出并落回同一高度、忽略空气阻力的抛体，初始仰角既不为 $0^\circ$ 也不为 $90^\circ$。（a）加速度是否会在某个时刻为零？（b）加速度是否会与速度的某个分量同向？（c）加速度是否会与速度的某个分量反向？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00005

Answer the following questions for projectile motion on level ground assuming negligible air resistance (the initial angle being neither <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mtext>0º</mtext></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"0°"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 nor <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mtext>90º</mtext></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"90°"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

): (a) Is the acceleration ever zero? (b) Is the acceleration ever in the same direction as a component of velocity? (c) Is the acceleration ever opposite in direction to a component of velocity?

<a id="cp-00006"></a>

## CP-00006｜射箭为何通常选择较低弹道

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／等射程的发射角

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

当抛出初速大小固定时，射程由发射角决定；除最大射程外，通常存在两个发射角使射程相同。考虑风等影响射箭命中率的因素，解释为什么较小的发射角（更接近水平方向）往往更合适。什么情况下必须使用较大的发射角？为什么美式橄榄球中的弃踢球员通常使用较高的飞行轨迹？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00006

For a fixed initial speed, the range of a projectile is determined by the angle at which it is fired. For all but the maximum, there are two angles that give the same range. Considering factors that might affect the ability of an archer to hit a target, such as wind, explain why the smaller angle (closer to the horizontal) is preferable. When would it be necessary for the archer to use the larger angle? Why does the punter in a football game use the higher trajectory?

<a id="cp-00007"></a>

## CP-00007｜同时离桌的两枚硬币

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／水平抛射与自由落体

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

课堂演示中，教师把两枚硬币放在桌边：她将其中一枚沿水平方向弹离桌面，同时把另一枚轻轻推过桌沿。描述两枚硬币此后的运动，特别讨论它们是否会**同时落地**。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00007

During a lecture demonstration, a professor places two coins on the edge of a table. She then flicks one of the coins horizontally off the table, simultaneously nudging the other over the edge. Describe the subsequent motion of the two coins, in particular discussing whether they hit the floor at the same time.

## 来源

- 原件：external_sources/college_physics/raw/CP-00007.md
- 原件 Git blob SHA：c6e7baf77cb6da0bb7f9b7a29b589bd8a5b28ef4
- 上游署名与许可证见原件；尚未完成单题权利审查。
- 审校提示：两枚硬币应从相同高度、同时开始离桌且竖直初速度相同，空气阻力忽略。

<a id="cp-00008"></a>

## CP-00008｜三秒后击中高处目标的弹体位移

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／位移分量

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一个抛射体从地面出发，初速为 $50.0\,\mathrm{m/s}$，发射方向与水平面成 $30.0^\circ$ 仰角。它在 $3.00\,\mathrm{s}$ 后击中高于地面的目标。求目标相对于发射点的水平位移 $x$ 和竖直位移 $y$。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00008

A projectile is launched at ground level with an initial speed of 50.0 m/s at an angle of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>30.0º</mn></mrow></semantics></math>

 above the horizontal. It strikes a target above the ground 3.00 seconds later. What are the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{x} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{y} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 distances from where the projectile was launched to where it lands?

<a id="cp-00009"></a>

## CP-00009｜由初速度分量求足球的落地速率

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／运动时间与最大高度

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一只足球被踢出时的初速度具有水平分量 $16\,\mathrm{m/s}$、竖直向上分量 $12\,\mathrm{m/s}$。（a）足球落地时的速率多大？（b）足球在空中运动多长时间？（c）达到的最大高度是多少？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00009

A ball is kicked with an initial velocity of 16 m/s in the horizontal direction and 12 m/s in the vertical direction. (a) At what speed does the ball hit the ground? (b) For how long does the ball remain in the air? (c)What maximum height is attained by the ball?

<a id="cp-00010"></a>

## CP-00010｜从高楼水平抛出的球

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／水平抛射

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一只球从高 $60.0\,\mathrm{m}$ 的楼顶水平抛出，落在距楼底 $100.0\,\mathrm{m}$ 处，忽略空气阻力。（a）球在空中停留多久？（b）初速度的水平分量多大？（c）落地前一瞬间速度的竖直分量是多少？（d）求落地前瞬间的**速度矢量**，同时给出水平与竖直分量。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00010

A ball is thrown horizontally from the top of a 60.0-m building and lands 100.0 m from the base of the building. Ignore air resistance. (a) How long is the ball in the air? (b) What must have been the initial horizontal component of the velocity? (c) What is the vertical component of the velocity just before the ball hits the ground? (d) What is the velocity (including both the horizontal and vertical components) of the ball just before it hits the ground?

<a id="cp-00011"></a>

## CP-00011｜摩托车飞越一排公共汽车

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／水平射程

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

（a）一位特技骑手驾驶摩托车以 $40.0\,\mathrm{m/s}$（$144\,\mathrm{km/h}$）的速率冲上仰角为 $32^\circ$ 的坡道，试图跃过首尾相接停放的一排公共汽车。已知起跳坡道顶部与车顶等高，每辆汽车长 $20.0\,\mathrm{m}$。不计空气阻力，他最多能越过多少辆车？（b）讨论这一表演的安全余量：实际射程比恰好越过最后一辆车所需的水平距离多出多少？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00011

(a) A daredevil is attempting to jump his motorcycle over a line of buses parked end to end by driving up a <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mtext>32º</mtext></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"32°"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 ramp at a speed of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext>40</mtext><mtext>.</mtext><mtext>0 m/s </mtext><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mtext>144 km/h</mtext><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"40" "." "0 m/s " \( "144 km/h" \) } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

. How many buses can he clear if the top of the takeoff ramp is at the same height as the bus tops and the buses are 20.0 m long? (b) Discuss what your answer implies about the margin of error in this act—that is, consider how much greater the range is than the horizontal distance he must travel to miss the end of the last bus. (Neglect air resistance.)

<a id="cp-00012"></a>

## CP-00012｜射箭越过树枝并击中靶心

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／双发射角与障碍

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一名射手向 $75.0\,\mathrm{m}$ 外的箭靶射箭，靶心与箭离弦处等高。（a）若箭的初速为 $35.0\,\mathrm{m/s}$，求命中靶心所需的发射角，并明确展示解抛体运动题的步骤。（b）在射手与靶之间的中点处有一棵大树，横向伸出的树枝比发射高度高 $3.50\,\mathrm{m}$。箭将从树枝上方还是下方经过？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00012

An archer shoots an arrow at a 75.0 m distant target; the bull’s-eye of the target is at same height as the release height of the arrow. (a) At what angle must the arrow be released to hit the bull’s-eye if its initial speed is 35.0 m/s? In this part of the problem, explicitly show how you follow the steps involved in solving projectile motion problems. (b) There is a large tree halfway between the archer and the target with an overhanging horizontal branch 3.50 m above the release height of the arrow. Will the arrow go over or under the branch?

<a id="cp-00013"></a>

## CP-00013｜相同高度接住的橄榄球传球

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／橄榄球传球

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一名橄榄球运动员把球传到横向相隔 $7.00\,\mathrm{m}$ 的队友手中，接球高度与出手高度相同。（a）已知初速为 $12.0\,\mathrm{m/s}$，若选用两种可行角度中的较小角度，求出手仰角；（b）另一种可达到相同水平射程的角度是多少，为什么通常不采用它？（c）球飞行了多长时间？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00013

A rugby player passes the ball 7.00 m across the field, where it is caught at the same height as it left his hand. (a) At what angle was the ball thrown if its initial speed was 12.0 m/s, assuming that the smaller of the two possible angles was used? (b) What other angle gives the same range, and why would it not be used? (c) How long did this pass take?

<a id="cp-00014"></a>

## CP-00014｜舰炮最大射程与地球曲率的影响

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／射程和地球曲率

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一艘战舰上的舰炮最远可将炮弹射至 $32.0\,\mathrm{km}$。（a）计算炮弹的初速度大小；（b）求炮弹达到的最大高度。（原文指出炮弹到达最高点时已高于大气层高度的 $60\%$，因此忽略空气阻力只是一种教学简化。）（c）海平面因地球曲率而非平面。设地球半径为 $6.37\times10^3\,\mathrm{km}$，在距战舰水平方向 $32.0\,\mathrm{km}$ 的位置，球形海面比战舰处的水平切线低多少米？这一差值是否足以影响前面按平面地球计算的结果？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00014

The cannon on a battleship can fire a shell a maximum distance of 32.0 km. (a) Calculate the initial velocity of the shell. (b) What maximum height does it reach? (At its highest, the shell is above 60% of the atmosphere—but air resistance is not really negligible as assumed to make this problem easier.) (c) The ocean is not flat, because the Earth is curved. Assume that the radius of the Earth is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><mtext>.</mtext><mrow><mtext>37</mtext><mo stretchy="false">×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mspace width="0.25em" /><mtext> km</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{6 "." "37" times "10" rSup { size 8{3} } " km"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

. How many meters lower will its surface be 32.0 km from the ship along a horizontal line parallel to the surface at the ship? Does your answer imply that error introduced by the assumption of a flat Earth in projectile motion is significant here?

## 来源

- 原件：external_sources/college_physics/raw/CP-00014.md

## CP-00015｜箭射向悬崖顶端

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／不等高抛射

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一支箭从离地 $1.5\,\mathrm{m}$ 高处射向高度为 $H$ 的悬崖，初速 $30\,\mathrm{m/s}$，仰角为 $60^\circ$。箭在 $4.0\,\mathrm{s}$ 后落在悬崖顶端边缘。（a）求悬崖高度 $H$；（b）求箭沿运动轨迹达到的最大高度；（c）求箭击中悬崖前一瞬间的速率。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00015

An arrow is shot from a height of 1.5 m toward a cliff of height <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{H} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

. It is shot with a velocity of 30 m/s at an angle of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mtext>60º</mtext></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"60°"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 above the horizontal. It lands on the top edge of the cliff 4.0 s later. (a) What is the height of the cliff? (b) What is the maximum height reached by the arrow along its trajectory? (c) What is the arrow’s impact speed just before hitting the cliff?

## CP-00016｜立定跳远的起跳加速与射程

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／立定跳远

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

立定跳远时，运动员先下蹲，再伸腿蹬地起跳。假设从屈膝姿势到完全伸腿的伸展距离为 $0.600\,\mathrm{m}$，在此阶段产生的加速度大小为重力加速度 $g$ 的 $1.25$ 倍。估算跳远距离，并清楚写出采用的假设。（原题指出，向起跳方向摆臂可进一步增加射程。）

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00016

In the standing broad jump, one squats and then pushes off with the legs to see how far one can jump. Suppose the extension of the legs from the crouch position is 0.600 m and the acceleration achieved from this position is 1.25 times the acceleration due to gravity, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{g} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

. How far can they jump? State your assumptions. (Increased range can be achieved by swinging the arms in the direction of the jump.)

## CP-00017｜跳远纪录与理想抛体最大射程

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／最大射程

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

男子跳远世界纪录为 $8.95\,\mathrm{m}$（迈克·鲍威尔，美国，1991 年）。若把跳远运动员视为抛体，起跳速率为 $9.5\,\mathrm{m/s}$，理论上可达到的最大水平射程是多少？请列出假设。

# Projectile Motion — CP-00017

The world long jump record is 8.95 m (Mike Powell, USA, 1991). Treated as a projectile, what is the maximum range obtainable by a person if he has a take-off speed of 9.5 m/s? State your assumptions.

## CP-00018｜网球发球的仰俯角与落点

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／过网与落点

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一名网球运动员以 $170\,\mathrm{km/h}$ 的速率发球，击球点离地 $2.5\,\mathrm{m}$，球向水平方向下方以 $\theta$ 角飞出。底线距网 $11.9\,\mathrm{m}$，网高 $0.91\,\mathrm{m}$。（a）求使网球**恰好越过球网**的 $\theta$；（b）球是否会落入发球区？已知发球线距网 $6.40\,\mathrm{m}$。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00018

Serving at a speed of 170 km/h, a tennis player hits the ball at a height of 2.5 m and an angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{θ} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 below the horizontal. The base line is 11.9 m from the net, which is 0.91 m high. What is the angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{θ} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 such that the ball just crosses the net? Will the ball land in the service box, whose service line is 6.40 m from the net?

## CP-00019｜后退中的四分卫传球

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／传球速度

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

美式橄榄球四分卫正以 $2.00\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 的速率直线后退，同时将球传给前方 $18.0\,\mathrm{m}$ 的队员。球相对于地面的发射角为 $25^\circ$，接球高度与出手高度相同。（a）求球相对**地面**的初速度大小；（b）求球到达接球者的时间；（c）求球高于出手位置的最大高度。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00019

A football quarterback is moving straight backward at a speed of 2.00 m/s when he throws a pass to a player 18.0 m straight downfield. (a) If the ball is thrown at an angle of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mtext>25º</mtext></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"25°"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 relative to the ground and is caught at the same height as it is released, what is its initial speed relative to the ground? (b) How long does it take to get to the receiver? (c) What is its maximum height above its point of release?

<a id="cp-00020"></a>

## CP-00020｜不同距离目标的弹道竖直偏差

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／重力造成的落点偏差

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

枪械瞄准具通常需要预先调高瞄准方向，以补偿重力造成的下坠，因此只能针对特定射程校准。（a）假设瞄准具已校准，使弹体恰好命中与枪口同高、距离 $100.0\,\mathrm{m}$ 的目标。若仍用相同瞄准设置对准 $150.0\,\mathrm{m}$ 外的同高度目标，弹体会命中目标下方多少距离？弹体初速为 $275\,\mathrm{m/s}$。（b）定性讨论更大的枪口初速以及空气阻力对上述结果的影响。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00020

Gun sights are adjusted to aim high to compensate for the effect of gravity, effectively making the gun accurate only for a specific range. (a) If a gun is sighted to hit targets that are at the same height as the gun and 100.0 m away, how low will the bullet hit if aimed directly at a target 150.0 m away? The muzzle velocity of the bullet is 275 m/s. (b) Discuss qualitatively how a larger muzzle velocity would affect this problem and what would be the effect of air resistance.

## CP-00022｜猫头鹰松开猎物能否落入巢中

 below the horizontal when it accidentally drops the mouse. Is the owl lucky enough to have the mouse hit the nest? To answer this question, calculate the horizontal position of the mouse when it has fallen 12.0 m.

<a id="cp-00025"></a>

## CP-00025｜篮球罚球恰好命中篮筐的角度

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／投篮发射角

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

篮球罚球线距篮筐 $4.57\,\mathrm{m}$（$15\,\mathrm{ft}$），篮筐离地 $3.05\,\mathrm{m}$（$10\,\mathrm{ft}$）。一名球员站在罚球线上，以 $8.15\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 的初速将球从离地 $2.44\,\mathrm{m}$（$8\,\mathrm{ft}$）处投出。球须以多大仰角出手才能恰好到达篮筐？注意：多数球员倾向于较陡的弹道，以获得更大的容错余量。请明确展示解决抛体运动问题的步骤。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00025

The free throw line in basketball is 4.57 m (15 ft) from the basket, which is 3.05 m (10 ft) above the floor. A player standing on the free throw line throws the ball with an initial speed of 8.15 m/s, releasing it at a height of 2.44 m (8 ft) above the floor. At what angle above the horizontal must the ball be thrown to exactly hit the basket? Note that most players will use a large initial angle rather than a flat shot because it allows for a larger margin of error. Explicitly show how you follow the steps involved in solving projectile motion problems.

## CP-00026｜铅球投掷初速度的反推

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／不同起落高度

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

原题称：2007 年，美国的 Michael Carter 以 $24.77\,\mathrm{m}$ 的成绩创造铅球世界纪录。若他从离地 $2.10\,\mathrm{m}$ 高的位置，以相对水平方向 $38.0^\circ$ 的仰角投出铅球，求铅球出手时的速度大小。（原题附注：在忽略空气阻力、同高起落的理想抛体模型中最大射程对应 $45^\circ$，但实际铅球投掷的最佳角度较小，因而 $38^\circ$ 可能比 $45^\circ$ 有更远射程。）

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00026

In 2007, Michael Carter (U.S.) set a world record in the shot put with a throw of 24.77 m. What was the initial speed of the shot if he released it at a height of 2.10 m and threw it at an angle of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>38.0º</mn></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"38"º} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 above the horizontal? (Although the maximum distance for a projectile on level ground is achieved at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext>45º</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"45"º} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 when air resistance is neglected, the actual angle to achieve maximum range is smaller; thus, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext>38º</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"38"º} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 will give a longer range than <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext>45º</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"45"º} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 in the shot put.)

## CP-00027｜篮球运动员起跳扣篮的速度与距离

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／起跳竖直速度

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一名篮球运动员以 $5.00\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 的速率朝篮筐直线奔跑，跳起准备扣篮；腾空时保持原有的水平速度。（a）若要使自己上升到离地 $0.750\,\mathrm{m}$ 的高度，竖直方向初速度需要多大？（b）若希望到达篮筐时恰好达到最高点，应在距篮筐多少水平距离处起跳？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00027

A basketball player is running at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mtext>.</mtext><mtext>00 m/s</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{5 "." "00 m/s"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 directly toward the basket when he jumps into the air to dunk the ball. He maintains his horizontal velocity. (a) What vertical velocity does he need to rise 0.750 m above the floor? (b) How far from the basket (measured in the horizontal direction) must he start his jump to reach his maximum height at the same time as he reaches the basket?

## 来源

- 原件：external_sources/college_physics/raw/CP-00027.md
- 原件 Git blob SHA：126e0579041b84b9525d9237bc47387af71d7ec1

## CP-00028｜阵风改变美式橄榄球水平射程

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／短时阵风扰动

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一名美式橄榄球运动员以 $45.0^\circ$ 的仰角将球踢出。若没有风，球的水平射程为 $60.0\,\mathrm{m}$。（a）求球的初速度大小；（b）当球接近最高点时，突然出现一阵短时强风，使球的水平速度减小 $1.50\,\mathrm{m/s}$。此后球在水平方向共飞行了多远？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00028

A football player punts the ball at a <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>45.0º</mn></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"45"°} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 angle. Without an effect from the wind, the ball would travel 60.0 m horizontally. (a) What is the initial speed of the ball? (b) When the ball is near its maximum height it experiences a brief gust of wind that reduces its horizontal velocity by 1.50 m/s. What distance does the ball travel horizontally?

<a id="cp-00029"></a>

## CP-00029｜证明无空气阻力的抛体轨迹是抛物线

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／轨迹方程推导

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

证明抛射体的运动轨迹是形如 $y=ax+bx^2$ 的抛物线。将水平运动方程 $x=v_{0x}t$ 解出时间 $t$，再代入竖直运动方程 $y=v_{0y}t-\tfrac12gt^2$。这些方程描述由坐标原点出发的抛体位置。最终应得到 $y=ax+bx^2$，其中 $a,b$ 为常数，并说明它们与初速度分量及重力加速度的关系。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00029

Prove that the trajectory of a projectile is parabolic, having the form <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">=</mo><mrow><mstyle fontstyle="italic"><mrow><mtext>ax</mtext></mrow></mstyle><mo stretchy="false">+</mo><mrow><msup><mstyle fontstyle="italic"><mtext>bx</mtext></mstyle><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{y= ital "ax"+ ital "bx" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

. To obtain this expression, solve the equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">=</mo><msub><mi>v</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></msub><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{x=v rSub { size 8{0x} } } {t}</annotation></semantics></math>

 for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{t} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 and substitute it into the expression for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">=</mo><msub><mi>v</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>–</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo stretchy="false">/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mrow><msup><mstyle fontstyle="italic"><mtext>gt</mtext></mstyle><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{y=υ rSub { size 8{0y} } t \( 1/2 \) ital "gt" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 (These equations describe the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{x} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{y} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 positions of a projectile that starts at the origin.) You should obtain an equation of the form <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">=</mo><mrow><mstyle fontstyle="italic"><mrow><mtext>ax</mtext></mrow></mstyle><mo stretchy="false">+</mo><mrow><msup><mstyle fontstyle="italic"><mtext>bx</mtext></mstyle><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{y= ital "ax"+ ital "bx" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{a} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{b} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 are constants.

<a id="cp-00030"></a>

## CP-00030｜从运动方程推导同高射程公式

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／射程公式推导

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

推导水平地面上抛射体的射程公式
$$R=\frac{v_0^2\sin(2\theta_0)}{g}.$$
先求出竖直位移 $y$ 再次变为零时的飞行时间 $t$，将该时刻代入水平位移 $x-x_0$ 的表达式，并利用 $R=x-x_0$ 说明结果。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00030

Derive <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi><mo stretchy="false">=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mspace width="0.25em" /><mtext>sin</mtext><mspace width="0.25em" /><msub><mn>2θ</mn><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mi>g</mi></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{R= { {v rSub { size 8{0} } rSup { size 8{2} } "sin"2θ rSub { size 8{0} } } over {g} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 for the range of a projectile on level ground by finding the time <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{t} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 at which <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{y} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 becomes zero and substituting this value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{t} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 into the expression for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">−</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{x - x rSub { size 8{0} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

, noting that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi><mo stretchy="false">=</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">−</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{R=x - x rSub { size 8{0} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

## CP-00031｜超高速发射情景中的模型适用性

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／不合理结果分析

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

**不合理结果题。**（a）某种超级火炮使弹体初速达到 $4.0\,\mathrm{km/s}$，按理想抛体公式求最大射程。（b）所得射程有何不合理之处？（c）问题出在初始设定不合理，还是所用方程已经不适用？解释。（d）若真的能达到这样的发射初速，定性讨论空气阻力、大气密度随高度减小以及地球曲率对射程的影响。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00031

(c) Is the premise unreasonable or is the available equation inapplicable? Explain your answer. (d) If such a muzzle velocity could be obtained, discuss the effects of air resistance, thinning air with altitude, and the curvature of the Earth on the range of the super cannon.

## CP-00032｜自拟抛球越过围栏的问题

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／自编建模题

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

**自拟问题。**设想一只球被抛过围栏。自行设计一道题，计算使球恰好越过围栏所需的初速度。需要自行确定的条件包括：围栏高度、出手点到围栏的水平距离和出手高度。进一步考虑：能否给定发射速率，只求所需发射角？对于你选定的高度和距离，是否可能存在多个解？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Projectile Motion — CP-00032

**Construct Your Own Problem** Consider a ball tossed over a fence. Construct a problem in which you calculate the ball’s needed initial velocity to just clear the fence. Among the things to determine are; the height of the fence, the distance to the fence from the point of release of the ball, and the height at which the ball is released. You should also consider whether it is possible to choose the initial speed for the ball and just calculate the angle at which it is thrown. Also examine the possibility of multiple solutions given the distances and heights you have chosen.

<a id="cp-00037"></a>

## CP-00037｜行驶卡车上落下的泥块

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 相对运动／速度方向转换

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一块泥土从行驶中卡车的车厢上掉下，落地点恰好位于车尾下方。它触地前一瞬间，**相对卡车**的速度方向如何？**相对地面**的速度方向是否相同？解释原因。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Addition of Velocities — CP-00037

A clod of dirt falls from the bed of a moving truck. It strikes the ground directly below the end of the truck. What is the direction of its velocity relative to the truck just before it hits? Is this the same as the direction of its velocity relative to ground just before it hits? Explain your answers.

## 来源

- 原件：external_sources/college_physics/raw/CP-00037.md
- 原件 Git blob SHA：b4574a6f0e1055ae38cd3661ea39d97d8e06bd06

<a id="cp-00038"></a>

## CP-00038｜人力飞机跨越英吉利海峡

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 相对运动／二维速度合成

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

1979 年 6 月 12 日，Bryan Allen 驾驶人力飞机，从英国多佛附近飞往法国格里内角。（a）他飞行 $169\,\mathrm{min}$，相对地面的平均速度大小为 $3.53\,\mathrm{m/s}$，方向是**东偏南 $45^\circ$**，求总位移。（b）途中平均逆风速率为 $2.00\,\mathrm{m/s}$，方向几乎与飞机对地运动方向完全相反，求飞机相对于空气的平均速度。（c）求飞机相对于空气团的总位移。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Addition of Velocities — CP-00038

Bryan Allen pedaled a human-powered aircraft across the English Channel from the cliffs of Dover to Cap Gris-Nez on June 12, 1979. (a) He flew for 169 min at an average velocity of 3.53 m/s in a direction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext>45º</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"45º"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 south of east. What was his total displacement? (b) Allen encountered a headwind averaging 2.00 m/s almost precisely in the opposite direction of his motion relative to the Earth. What was his average velocity relative to the air? (c) What was his total displacement relative to the air mass?

## CP-00039｜海鸥逆风与顺风飞行时间

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 相对运动／顺逆风往返时间

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一只海鸥以 $9.00\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 的速度正对风向飞行。（a）若它用 $20.0\,\mathrm{min}$ 飞过相对地面的 $6.00\,\mathrm{km}$，求风速。（b）若海鸥掉头顺风飞回 $6.00\,\mathrm{km}$，需多长时间？（c）讨论与无风时相比，风如何影响往返所需的总时间。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Addition of Velocities — CP-00039

A seagull flies at a velocity of 9.00 m/s straight into the wind. (a) If it takes the bird 20.0 min to travel 6.00 km relative to the Earth, what is the velocity of the wind? (b) If the bird turns around and flies with the wind, how long will he take to return 6.00 km? (c) Discuss how the wind affects the total round-trip time compared to what it would be with no wind.

## 来源

- 原件：external_sources/college_physics/raw/CP-00039.md

## CP-00040｜马拉松终点前两名选手的追及

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 相对运动／追及问题

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

马拉松比赛临近终点时，领先与第二名选手相隔 $45.0\,\mathrm{m}$，领先者速度为 $3.50\,\mathrm{m/s}$，第二名速度为 $4.20\,\mathrm{m/s}$。（a）求第二名相对领先者的速度；（b）如果领先者距终点还剩 $250\,\mathrm{m}$，假定两人均保持恒速，谁会赢得比赛？（c）胜者越过终点时，领先另一人多少米？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Addition of Velocities — CP-00040

Near the end of a marathon race, the first two runners are separated by a distance of 45.0 m. The front runner has a velocity of 3.50 m/s, and the second a velocity of 4.20 m/s. (a) What is the velocity of the second runner relative to the first? (b) If the front runner is 250 m from the finish line, who will win the race, assuming they run at constant velocity? (c) What distance ahead will the winner be when she crosses the finish line?

美式橄榄球四分卫以 $2.00\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 的速率向后直线运动，同时把球传给前方 $18.0\,\mathrm{m}$ 的队员。球相对地面的发射角为 $25.0^\circ$，接球高度与出手高度相同。求球**相对四分卫本人**的初速度矢量。

# Addition of Velocities — CP-00041

A football quarterback is moving straight backward at a speed of 2.00 m/s when he throws a pass to a player 18.0 m straight downfield. The ball is thrown at an angle of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>25.0º</mn></mrow></semantics></math>

 relative to the ground and is caught at the same height as it is released. What is the initial velocity of the ball *relative to the quarterback* ?

 north of east. What is the velocity of the ship relative to the Earth?

## CP-00043｜喷气式飞机在高空急流中的对地速度

## 中文译题

（a）一架从澳大利亚达尔文起飞的喷气式飞机，空速为 $260\,\mathrm{m/s}$，相对于正西方向偏南 $5.0^\circ$。飞机进入高空急流风带，风速 $35.0\,\mathrm{m/s}$，方向为**东偏南 $15^\circ$**。求飞机相对地面的速度。（b）讨论计算结果是否符合风对飞机航迹影响的直觉。

# Addition of Velocities — CP-00043

(a) A jet airplane flying from Darwin, Australia, has an air speed of 260 m/s in a direction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>5.0º</mn></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"15"°} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 south of west. It is in the jet stream, which is blowing at 35.0 m/s in a direction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>15º</mn></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"15"°} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 south of east. What is the velocity of the airplane relative to the Earth? (b) Discuss whether your answers are consistent with your expectations for the effect of the wind on the plane’s path.

 east of north relative to the Earth.  south of west relative to the Earth. What is the velocity of the wind relative to the water?

 west of north at a speed of 4.00 m/s relative to the water. ## CP-00048｜向高轨道宇航员竖直投送补给是否合理

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 抛体运动／极端高度下的模型失效

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

**不合理结果题。**设想你要把补给物资从地面**竖直发射**给距地表 $36{,}000\,\mathrm{km}$ 轨道上的宇航员。（a）所需发射速度多大？（b）算出的速度有何不合理？（c）当补给物资到达其最高点时，它与宇航员之间的相对速度是否构成问题？（d）是不合理的任务前提，还是现有抛体运动方程不再适用？解释。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Addition of Velocities — CP-00048

**Unreasonable Results** Suppose you wish to shoot supplies straight up to astronauts in an orbit 36,000 km above the surface of the Earth. (a) At what velocity must the supplies be launched? (b) What is unreasonable about this velocity? (c) Is there a problem with the relative velocity between the supplies and the astronauts when the supplies reach their maximum height? (d) Is the premise unreasonable or is the available equation inapplicable? Explain your answer.

## CP-00049｜飞机极大对地航速与风速的合理性

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 相对运动／不合理数据分析

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

**不合理结果题。**一架民航客机相对空气以 $280\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 的速率**正东**飞行，且有强顺风。飞机在 $1.50\,\mathrm{h}$ 内向**东偏南 $5^\circ$** 的方向飞行了 $3000\,\mathrm{km}$。（a）求飞机的对地速度；（b）求所需顺风的速度大小及方向；（c）上述两个速度有何不合理之处？（d）哪项题设假定不合理？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Addition of Velocities — CP-00049

**Unreasonable Results** A commercial airplane has an air speed of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mtext>280 m/s</mtext></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"280 m/s"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 due east and flies with a strong tailwind. It travels 3000 km in a direction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>5º</mn></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{5°} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 south of east in 1.50 h. (a) What was the velocity of the plane relative to the ground? (b) Calculate the magnitude and direction of the tailwind’s velocity. (c) What is unreasonable about both of these velocities? (d) Which premise is unreasonable?

## CP-00050｜自拟横风中飞机对正跑道的航向问题

## 中文译题

**自拟问题。**设想飞机在横风中接近跑道，自行设计一道题，计算飞行员应使飞机相对空气朝向什么角度，才能使对地速度方向与跑道平行。需要确定跑道方向、风的速率和方向，以及飞机相对空气的速率；还要计算飞机的对地速率。讨论飞机接地前可能需要进行哪些修正操作，才能让机轮沿跑道方向指向前方。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Addition of Velocities — CP-00050

**Construct Your Own Problem** Consider an airplane headed for a runway in a cross wind. Construct a problem in which you calculate the angle the airplane must fly relative to the air mass in order to have a velocity parallel to the runway. Among the things to consider are the direction of the runway, the wind speed and direction (its velocity) and the speed of the plane relative to the air mass. Also calculate the speed of the airplane relative to the ground. Discuss any last minute maneuvers the pilot might have to perform in order for the plane to land with its wheels pointing straight down the runway.

Suppose a net external force of 50.0 N is exerted and the astronaut’s acceleration is measured to be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mtext>.</mtext><msup><mtext>893 m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{0 "." . (a) Calculate her mass. (b) By exerting a force on the astronaut, the vehicle in which they orbit experiences an equal and opposite force. Discuss how this would affect the measurement of the astronaut’s acceleration. Propose a method in which recoil of the vehicle is avoided.

## CP-00067｜两个孩子推车上第三名孩子

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 牛顿第二定律／相反方向合力与摩擦

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

两个孩子沿水平方向、以**完全相反的方向**推动载有第三名孩子的小车：第一名施力 $75.0\,\mathrm{N}$，第二名施力 $90.0\,\mathrm{N}$，摩擦力大小为 $12.0\,\mathrm{N}$，车上孩子及小车总质量为 $23.0\,\mathrm{kg}$。（a）若要计算车上孩子的加速度，研究系统应如何选取？（b）画出系统所受全部力的受力分析图；（c）求加速度；（d）若摩擦力变为 $15.0\,\mathrm{N}$，加速度是多少？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Newton’s Second Law of Motion: Concept of a System — CP-00067

Suppose two children push horizontally, but in exactly opposite directions, on a third child in a wagon. The first child exerts a force of 75.0 N, the second a force of 90.0 N, friction is 12.0 N, and the mass of the third child plus wagon is 23.0 kg. (a) What is the system of interest if the acceleration of the child in the wagon is to be calculated? (b) Draw a free-body diagram, including all forces acting on the system. (c) Calculate the acceleration. (d) What would the acceleration be if friction were 15.0 N?

## CP-00068｜摩托车加速时对地面的作用力

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 牛顿第二定律／轮胎牵引力

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一辆高性能摩托车以 $90.0\,\mathrm{km/h}$ 的速率行驶时，可产生 $3.50\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$ 的加速度。此速率下，包括摩擦阻力和空气阻力在内的总阻力为 $400\,\mathrm{N}$。摩托车与骑手的总质量为 $245\,\mathrm{kg}$。为获得上述加速度，摩托车对地面**向后**施加的力有多大？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Newton’s Second Law of Motion: Concept of a System — CP-00068

A powerful motorcycle can produce an acceleration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>3.50</mn><mspace width="0.25em" /><msup><mtext> m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{3 "." "50"" m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 while traveling at 90.0 km/h. At that speed the forces resisting motion, including friction and air resistance, total 400 N. (Air resistance is analogous to air friction. It always opposes the motion of an object.) What is the magnitude of the force the motorcycle exerts backward on the ground to produce its acceleration if the mass of the motorcycle with rider is 245 kg?

<a id="cp-00080"></a>

## CP-00080｜火箭的加速度与受力分析

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 牛顿运动定律／竖直运动

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一枚质量为 $5.00\times10^5\,\mathrm{kg}$ 的火箭正在竖直向上加速。发动机产生 $1.250\times10^7\,\mathrm{N}$ 的推力，空气阻力为 $4.50\times10^6\,\mathrm{N}$。求火箭的加速度，并明确展示如何依照牛顿运动定律的解题步骤进行分析。

"250" times "10" rSup { size 8{7} } " N"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 of thrust, and air resistance is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mtext>.</mtext><mrow><mtext>50</mtext><mo stretchy="false">×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mspace width="0.25em" /><mtext> N</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{4 "." "50" times "10" rSup { size 8{6} } " N"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

. What is the rocket’s acceleration? Explicitly show how you follow the steps in the Problem-Solving Strategy for Newton’s laws of motion.

<a id="cp-00081"></a>

## CP-00081｜汽车质量与受力分析

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 牛顿第二定律／汽车牵引力和阻力

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一辆中型汽车的车轮对路面施加向后 $2100\,\mathrm{N}$ 的力，从而使汽车向前加速。包括空气阻力在内的阻力大小为 $250\,\mathrm{N}$，汽车加速度为 $1.80\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$。求汽车连同乘客的总质量。请按牛顿运动定律的解题步骤作答，并绘制受力分析图、写出合外力方程。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Problem-Solving Strategies — CP-00081

The wheels of a midsize car exert a force of 2100 N backward on the road to accelerate the car in the forward direction. If the force of friction including air resistance is 250 N and the acceleration of the car is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext>.</mtext><msup><mtext>80 m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{1 "." "80 m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

, what is the mass of the car plus its occupants? Explicitly show how you follow the steps in the Problem-Solving Strategy for Newton’s laws of motion. For this situation, draw a free-body diagram and write the net force equation.

## CP-00082｜跳高运动员为产生竖直加速度所需的蹬地力

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 牛顿第二定律／跳高起跳反作用力

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一名质量为 $70.0\,\mathrm{kg}$ 的跳高运动员要产生大小为重力加速度 $g$ 的 $4.00$ 倍、方向向上的加速度。他必须对地面施加多大的力？请明确展示运用牛顿运动定律的解题步骤。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Problem-Solving Strategies — CP-00082

Calculate the force a 70.0-kg high jumper must exert on the ground to produce an upward acceleration 4.00 times the acceleration due to gravity. Explicitly show how you follow the steps in the Problem-Solving Strategy for Newton’s laws of motion.

- 原件：external_sources/college_physics/raw/CP-00082.md

<a id="cp-00083"></a>

## CP-00083｜体操运动员着地时对垫子的作用力

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 动力学／落地减速受力

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一名质量为 $40.0\,\mathrm{kg}$ 的体操运动员完成空翻后落地，通过向下压垫子使身体减速。若其减速度大小为重力加速度的 $7.00$ 倍，求她必须对垫子施加的力，并明确展示牛顿运动定律的解题步骤。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Problem-Solving Strategies — CP-00083

When landing after a spectacular somersault, a 40.0-kg gymnast decelerates by pushing straight down on the mat. Calculate the force she must exert if her deceleration is 7.00 times the acceleration due to gravity. Explicitly show how you follow the steps in the Problem-Solving Strategy for Newton’s laws of motion.

## CP-00084｜货运列车的牵引力和车钩张力

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 动力学／列车牵引与车厢连接力

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一列货运列车由两台质量各为 $8.00\times10^4\,\mathrm{kg}$ 的机车和 $45$ 节平均质量各为 $5.50\times10^4\,\mathrm{kg}$ 的车厢组成。（a）若列车要获得 $5.00\times10^{-2}\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$ 的加速度，总摩擦阻力为 $7.50\times10^5\,\mathrm{N}$，假定两台机车提供相同的牵引力，求**每台机车对铁轨向后施加的力**。（b）假定每节车厢质量相同，且摩擦阻力均匀分布在全部车厢与机车之间，求第 $37$ 和第 $38$ 节车厢之间车钩的作用力（即两节车厢对彼此的力）。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Problem-Solving Strategies — CP-00084

A freight train consists of two <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mn>8.00</mn> <mo>× </mo> <msup> <mn>10</mn> <mn>4</mn> </msup> <mtext>-kg</mtext> </math>

 engines and 45 cars with average masses of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mn>5.50</mn> <mo>× </mo> <msup> <mn>10</mn> <mn>4</mn> </msup><mspace width="0.25em" /> <mtext>kg</mtext> </math>

 . (a) What force must each engine exert backward on the track to accelerate the train at a rate of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>5.00</mn><mrow><mo stretchy="false">×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mtext>–2</mtext></mrow></msup></mrow><mspace width="0.25em" /><msup><mtext> m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{5 "." "00" times "10" rSup { size 8{"–2"} } " m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 if the force of friction is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn><mtext>.</mtext><mrow><mtext>50</mtext><mo stretchy="false">×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mspace width="0.25em" /><mtext> N</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{7 "." "50" times "10" rSup { size 8{5} } " N"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

, assuming the engines exert identical forces? This is not a large frictional force for such a massive system. Rolling friction for trains is small, and consequently trains are very energy-efficient transportation systems. (b) What is the force in the coupling between the 37th and 38th cars (this is the force each exerts on the other), assuming all cars have the same mass and that friction is evenly distributed among all of the cars and engines?

## CP-00085｜牵引车推动客机时的质量与作用力

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 动力学／牵引车和飞机

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

民航飞机有时由牵引车从登机区推出。（a）一辆质量 $1800\,\mathrm{kg}$ 的牵引车对路面向后施加 $1.75\times10^4\,\mathrm{N}$ 的力，整个系统受到的总阻力为 $2400\,\mathrm{N}$，加速度为 $0.150\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$。求飞机质量。（b）若飞机自身承受的摩擦阻力为 $2200\,\mathrm{N}$，求牵引车对飞机的作用力。（c）画出两幅示意图，分别展示两问采用的研究系统及其受力分析图。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Problem-Solving Strategies — CP-00085

Commercial airplanes are sometimes pushed out of the passenger loading area by a tractor. (a) An 1800-kg tractor exerts a force of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext>.</mtext><mrow><mtext>75</mtext><mo stretchy="false">×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mspace width="0.25em" /><mtext> N</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{1 "." "75" times "10" rSup { size 8{4 } } " N"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 backward on the pavement, and the system experiences forces resisting motion that total 2400 N. If the acceleration is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mtext>.</mtext><msup><mtext>150 m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{0 "." "150 m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

, what is the mass of the airplane? (b) Calculate the force exerted by the tractor on the airplane, assuming 2200 N of the friction is experienced by the airplane. (c) Draw two sketches showing the systems of interest used to solve each part, including the free-body diagrams for each.

## CP-00086｜汽车拖船时的阻力和牵引钩受力

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 动力学／拖车耦合受力

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一辆质量为 $1100\,\mathrm{kg}$ 的汽车牵引着载有船只的拖车。（a）汽车对道路向后施加 $1900\,\mathrm{N}$ 的力，整个系统以 $0.550\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$ 的加速度前进。若船只与拖车总质量为 $700\,\mathrm{kg}$，求汽车、船只和拖车所受的**总阻力**。（b）若总阻力的 $80\%$ 作用在船只与拖车上，求汽车与拖车之间的连接钩作用力。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Problem-Solving Strategies — CP-00086

A 1100-kg car pulls a boat on a trailer. (a) What total force resists the motion of the car, boat, and trailer, if the car exerts a 1900-N force on the road and produces an acceleration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mtext>.</mtext><msup><mtext>550 m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{0 "." "550 m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

? The mass of the boat plus trailer is 700 kg. (b) What is the force in the hitch between the car and the trailer if 80% of the resisting forces are experienced by the boat and trailer?

## CP-00087｜护士斜向下推动手推车

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 动力学／斜向推力与摩擦

## 中文译题

一名护士通过把手推车施加与水平方向向下成 $35.0^\circ$ 的力。装载货物后手推车质量为 $28.0\,\mathrm{kg}$，摩擦阻力大小为 $60.0\,\mathrm{N}$。（a）绘制研究系统的受力分析图；（b）为了使手推车以恒定速度运动，护士需要施加多大的推力？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Problem-Solving Strategies — CP-00087

A nurse pushes a cart by exerting a force on the handle at a downward angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext>35.0º</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"35" "." 0°} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 below the horizontal. The loaded cart has a mass of 28.0 kg, and the force of friction is 60.0 N. (a) Draw a free-body diagram for the system of interest. (b) What force must the nurse exert to move at a constant velocity?

## CP-00088｜自拟升降机加速的缆绳张力问题

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 动力学／升降机缆绳拉力

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

**自拟问题。**考虑升降机由静止起步，并加速将载荷提升到某一巡航速度的过程。以升降机及载荷整体为研究系统，绘制受力分析图，再求缆绳张力。设计时需要考虑升降机和载荷的总质量、最终速度以及达到该速度所需的时间。

# Problem-Solving Strategies — CP-00088

**Construct Your Own Problem** Consider the tension in an elevator cable during the time the elevator starts from rest and accelerates its load upward to some cruising velocity. Taking the elevator and its load to be the system of interest, draw a free-body diagram. Then calculate the tension in the cable. Among the things to consider are the mass of the elevator and its load, the final velocity, and the time taken to reach that velocity.

## CP-00089｜自拟雪橇上坡加速问题

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 动力学／斜面受力与分解

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

**自拟问题。**两个人推动载有四名儿童的雪橇沿积雪斜坡向上运动。自行设计一道题，计算雪橇及其载荷的加速度，并以合适研究系统的受力分析图为依据。标出力矢量及分量并说明坐标选择；需要考虑推车人的施力、斜坡倾角、雪橇和儿童的质量等。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Problem-Solving Strategies — CP-00089

**Construct Your Own Problem** Consider two people pushing a toboggan with four children on it up a snow-covered slope. Construct a problem in which you calculate the acceleration of the toboggan and its load. Include a free-body diagram of the appropriate system of interest as the basis for your analysis. Show vector forces and their components and explain the choice of coordinates. Among the things to be considered are the forces exerted by those pushing, the angle of the slope, and the masses of the toboggan and children.

## CP-00090｜推力不足的火箭能否起飞

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 动力学／火箭推重比和不合理结果

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

**不合理结果题。**（a）一枚起飞时质量为 $1.50\times10^6\,\mathrm{kg}$ 的火箭，其发动机推力为 $2.00\times10^6\,\mathrm{N}$，求初始加速度，**不得忽略重力**。（b）结果有何不合理之处？（原题指出真实火箭也曾意外出现类似情况。）（c）哪些前提不合理或相互矛盾？可以与本节较前面的火箭题比较。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Problem-Solving Strategies — CP-00090

**Unreasonable Results** (a) What is the initial acceleration of a rocket that has a mass of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext>.</mtext><mrow><mtext>50</mtext><mo stretchy="false">×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mspace width="0.25em" /><mtext> kg</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{1 "." "50" times "10" rSup { size 8{6} } " kg"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 at takeoff, the engines of which produce a thrust of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext>.</mtext><mrow><mtext>00</mtext><mo stretchy="false">×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mspace width="0.25em" /><mtext> N</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{2 "." "00" times "10" rSup { size 8{6} } " N"} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

? Do not neglect gravity. (b) What is unreasonable about the result? (This result has been unintentionally achieved by several real rockets.) (c) Which premise is unreasonable, or which premises are inconsistent? (You may find it useful to compare this problem to the rocket problem earlier in this section.)

## CP-00110｜足球踢球时的角速度、平均力和射程

## 中文译题

**综合概念题。**足球运动员踢球时腿部绕髋关节转动。（a）若鞋尖速率为 $35.0\,\mathrm{m/s}$，髋关节距鞋尖 $1.05\,\mathrm{m}$，求鞋尖的角速度；（b）鞋尖与初始静止、质量为 $0.500\,\mathrm{kg}$ 的足球接触 $20.0\,\mathrm{ms}$，使其速率达到 $20.0\,\mathrm{m/s}$，求对球施加的平均力；（c）忽略空气阻力，求球的最大水平射程。

# Rotation Angle and Angular Velocity — CP-00110

(b) The shoe is in contact with the initially stationary 0.500 kg football for 20.0 ms. What average force is exerted on the football to give it a velocity of 20.0 m/s?

(c) Find the maximum range of the football, neglecting air resistance.

## CP-00111｜自拟旋转筒乘客不下滑的角速度题

## 中文译题

**自拟问题。**设想游乐园中一种乘客靠在竖直圆筒内壁、随圆筒绕竖直轴旋转的游乐装置。当角速度达到一定数值后，底板下降，乘客仍被筒壁摩擦力托住而不下滑。设计一道计算所需最小角速度的题目，并绘制某位乘客的受力分析图。需要考虑圆筒半径及乘客衣物与筒壁之间的摩擦因数等因素。

**Construct Your Own Problem**

Consider an amusement park ride in which participants are rotated about a vertical axis in a cylinder with vertical walls. Once the angular velocity reaches its full value, the floor drops away and friction between the walls and the riders prevents them from sliding down. Construct a problem in which you calculate the necessary angular velocity that assures the riders will not slide down the wall. Include a free body diagram of a single rider. Among the variables to consider are the radius of the cylinder and the coefficients of friction between the riders’ clothing and the wall.

民航客机起飞时的速率为 $60.0\,\mathrm{m/s}$，轮胎直径为 $0.850\,\mathrm{m}$。（a）轮胎每分钟转多少圈？（b）轮胎边缘处的向心加速度是多少？（c）一只质量 $1.00\times10^{-15}\,\mathrm{kg}$ 的细菌若附着在轮胎边缘，需受到多大向心力？（d）求这一作用力与细菌重力的比值。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Centripetal Acceleration — CP-00122

At takeoff, a commercial jet has a 60.0 m/s speed. Its tires have a diameter of 0.850 m.

(a) At how many rev/min are the tires rotating?

(b) What is the centripetal acceleration at the edge of the tire?

(c) With what force must a determined <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext>.</mtext><mrow><mtext>00</mtext><mo stretchy="false">×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">−</mo><mtext>15</mtext></mrow></mrow></msup><mspace width="0.25em" /><mtext>kg</mtext></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{1 "." "00" times "10" rSup { size 8{ - "15"} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 bacterium cling to the rim?

(d) Take the ratio of this force to the bacterium’s weight.

<a id="cp-00123"></a>

## CP-00123｜维京船摆动游乐设施中的向心加速度

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 圆周运动／能量与底部支持力

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

**综合题。**一种维京船形游乐设施绕高处的固定支点前后摆动，近似为刚性摆。船在圆弧顶部某时刻短暂静止后，在重力作用下摆至底部。（a）若可忽略摩擦，船的质心沿半径为 $14.0\,\mathrm{m}$ 的圆弧运动，乘客靠近质心，求乘客到达圆弧最低点的速率；（b）求最低点的向心加速度；（c）画出最低点乘客的受力分析图；（d）求游乐设施对质量为 $60.0\,\mathrm{kg}$ 的乘客施加的力，并与其重力比较；（e）讨论结果是否合理。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Centripetal Acceleration — CP-00123

**Integrated Concepts**

Riders in an amusement park ride shaped like a Viking ship hung from a large pivot are rotated back and forth like a rigid pendulum. Sometime near the middle of the ride, the ship is momentarily motionless at the top of its circular arc. The ship then swings down under the influence of gravity.

(a) Assuming negligible friction, find the speed of the riders at the bottom of its arc, given the system's center of mass travels in an arc having a radius of 14.0 m and the riders are near the center of mass.

(b) What is the centripetal acceleration at the bottom of the arc?

(c) Draw a free body diagram of the forces acting on a rider at the bottom of the arc.

(d) Find the force exerted by the ride on a 60.0 kg rider and compare it to her weight.

(e) Discuss whether the answer seems reasonable.

## CP-00124｜儿童秋千最低点的向心力是否合理

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 圆周运动／摆动与不合理结果

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

**不合理结果题。**一名母亲推动秋千上的孩子，使孩子在轨迹最低点的速率达到 $9.00\,\mathrm{m/s}$。秋千悬挂点位于孩子质心上方 $2.00\,\mathrm{m}$。（a）求最低点孩子向心加速度大小；（b）若孩子质量为 $18.0\,\mathrm{kg}$，求孩子对座椅施加的力的大小；（c）为什么这些结果不合理？（d）哪些条件不合理或相互矛盾？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Centripetal Acceleration — CP-00124

**Unreasonable Results**

A mother pushes her child on a swing so that his speed is 9.00 m/s at the lowest point of his path. The swing is suspended 2.00 m above the child’s center of mass.

(a) What is the magnitude of the centripetal acceleration of the child at the low point?

(b) What is the magnitude of the force the child exerts on the seat if his mass is 18.0 kg?

(c) What is unreasonable about these results?

(d) Which premises are unreasonable or inconsistent?

## CP-00158｜里程表与速度表分别测什么

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 运动学／路程和速度的测量

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

## 中文译题

1934 年 $5$ 月 $26$ 日，流线型不锈钢柴油列车 Zephyr 创下长途不停站列车速度纪录。它从丹佛驶往芝加哥，耗时 $13$ 小时 $4$ 分 $58$ 秒，沿途据称有逾百万人见证，全程路程 $1633.8\,\mathrm{km}$。求其平均速率，分别以 $\mathrm{km/h}$ 和 $\mathrm{m/s}$ 表示。

# Time, Velocity, and Speed — CP-00165

On May 26, 1934, a streamlined, stainless steel diesel train called the Zephyr set the world’s nonstop long-distance speed record for trains. Its run from Denver to Chicago took 13 hours, 4 minutes, 58 seconds, and was witnessed by more than a million people along the route. The total distance traveled was 1633.8 km. What was its average speed in km/h and m/s?

(b) If the straight-line distance from her home to the university is 10.3 km in a direction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mtext>25</mtext><mtext>.</mtext><mn>0º</mn></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"25" "." 0°} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 south of east, what was her average velocity? (c) If she returned home by the same path 7 h 30 min after she left, what were her average speed and velocity for the entire trip?

"00" times "10" rSup { size 8{8} } " m/s" \) } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

.

A well-thrown ball is caught in a well-padded mitt. If the deceleration of the ball is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext>.</mtext><mtext>10</mtext><mo>×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mspace width="0.25em" /><msup><mtext> m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow></semantics></math>

, and 1.85 ms <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mtext>1 ms</mtext><mo stretchy="false">=</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">−</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></msup></mrow><mspace width="0.25em" /><mtext> s</mtext><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{ \( "1 ms"="10" rSup { size 8{-3} } " s" \) } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 elapses from the time the ball first touches the mitt until it stops, what was the initial velocity of the ball?

"20"´"10" rSup { size 8{5} } " m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><mtext>.</mtext><mtext>10</mtext><mo>×</mo><msup><mtext>10</mtext><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">−</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mrow></msup><mspace width="0.25em" /><mtext> s</mtext></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow></semantics></math>

. What is its muzzle velocity (that is, its final velocity)?

## 中文译题

. How long does it take to reach its top speed of 80.0 km/h, starting from rest? "65 m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

. How long does it take to come to a stop from its top speed? (c) In emergencies the train can decelerate more rapidly, coming to rest from 80.0 km/h in 8.30 s. What is its emergency deceleration in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><msup><mtext>m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{"m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

?

## 中文译题

一辆汽车准备驶入高速公路时，从静止开始以 $2.40\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$ 的加速度运动 $12.0\,\mathrm{s}$。（a）画出情景示意图；（b）列出题中已知量；（c）求这 $12.0\,\mathrm{s}$ 内行驶的距离，先列未知量、解释选用哪个方程，再展示计算步骤、检查单位并讨论答案是否合理；（d）按同样方式求末速度并展示完整步骤。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Motion Equations for Constant Acceleration in One Dimension — CP-00176

While entering a freeway, a car accelerates from rest at a rate of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext>.</mtext><msup><mtext>40 m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{2 "." "40 m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 for 12.0 s. (a) Draw a sketch of the situation. (b) List the knowns in this problem. (c) How far does the car travel in those 12.0 s? After choosing the equation, show your steps in solving for the unknown, check your units, and discuss whether the answer is reasonable. (d) What is the car’s final velocity? Solve for this unknown in the same manner as in part (c), showing all steps explicitly.

"00 m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

. (a) How far does she travel in the next 5.00 s? (b) What is her final velocity? (c) Evaluate the result. Does it make sense?

## 中文译题

**职业应用题。**血液在心脏左心室作用下由静止加速，在 $1.80\,\mathrm{cm}$ 的距离内达到 $30.0\,\mathrm{cm/s}$。（a）画出情景示意图；（b）列出已知量；（c）求加速历时，并依次说明待求量、方程选取、计算步骤和单位检查；（d）将所得时间与一次心跳的持续时间比较，判断结果是否合理。

After choosing the equation, show your steps in solving for the unknown, checking your units. (d) Is the answer reasonable when compared with the time for a heartbeat?

## CP-00184｜啄木鸟头部和大脑的减速

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 一维匀加速运动／碰撞减速

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

**职业应用题。**啄木鸟颅内的类似肌腱附着结构能够减轻大脑受急剧减速的影响。啄树时，啄木鸟头部以 $0.600\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 的初速度，在仅 $2.00\,\mathrm{mm}$ 的距离内停下。（a）求头部的减速度，分别用 $\mathrm{m/s^2}$ 和重力加速度 $g=9.80\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$ 的倍数表示；（b）求停止所需时间；（c）若保护大脑的结构拉伸，使大脑停止距离增至 $4.50\,\mathrm{mm}$，求大脑减速度为多少倍 $g$。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Motion Equations for Constant Acceleration in One Dimension — CP-00184

**Professional Application:**

A woodpecker’s brain is specially protected from large decelerations by tendon-like attachments inside the skull. While pecking on a tree, the woodpecker’s head comes to a stop from an initial velocity of 0.600 m/s in a distance of only 2.00 mm. (a) Find the acceleration in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><msup><mtext>m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow /></mrow></semantics></math>

<sup /> and in multiples of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi><mspace width="0.25em" /><mfenced open="(" close=")"><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo stretchy="false">=</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow><mtext>.</mtext><mtext>80</mtext><mspace width="0.25em" /><msup><mtext> m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{g left (g=9 "." "80"" m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } right )} {}</annotation></semantics></math>

. (b) Calculate the stopping time. (c) The tendons cradling the brain stretch, making its stopping distance 4.50 mm (greater than the head and, hence, less deceleration of the brain). What is the brain’s deceleration, expressed in multiples of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow></mrow></semantics></math>

?

## CP-00185｜运动员撞上带衬垫门柱时的减速度

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 一维匀加速运动／碰撞停止距离

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一名美式橄榄球运动员以 $7.50\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 的速率跑动，意外撞上装有缓冲垫的球门柱。缓冲垫与其身体共压缩 $0.350\,\mathrm{m}$ 后，他完全停止。（a）求减速度；（b）求碰撞持续时间。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Motion Equations for Constant Acceleration in One Dimension — CP-00185

An unwary football player collides with a padded goalpost while running at a velocity of 7.50 m/s and comes to a full stop after compressing the padding and his body 0.350 m. (a) What is his deceleration? (b) How long does the collision last?

<a id="cp-00186"></a>

## CP-00186｜飞行员高空坠落后由积雪和树木缓冲

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 一维匀加速运动／缓冲停止距离

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

原题记载：第二次世界大战期间，有飞行员为逃离着火飞机而未带降落伞跳下，部分人从约 $20{,}000\,\mathrm{ft}$（$6000\,\mathrm{m}$）的高度坠落后幸存。树枝与积雪使落地减速过程得以延长。设一名飞行员着地瞬间的速率为 $123\,\mathrm{mi/h}$（$54\,\mathrm{m/s}$），在树木与积雪作用下于 $3.0\,\mathrm{m}$ 距离内停下，求其减速度大小。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Motion Equations for Constant Acceleration in One Dimension — CP-00186

In World War II, there were several reported cases of airmen who jumped from their flaming airplanes with no parachute to escape certain death. Some fell about 20,000 feet (6000 m), and some of them survived, with few life-threatening injuries. For these lucky pilots, the tree branches and snow drifts on the ground allowed their deceleration to be relatively small. If we assume that a pilot’s speed upon impact was 123 mph (54 m/s), then what was his deceleration? Assume that the trees and snow stopped him over a distance of 3.0 m.

(b) If the squirrel stops in a distance of 2.0 cm through bending its limbs, compare its deceleration with that of the airman in the previous problem.

## CP-00188｜快车车头车尾通过站台的时间

## 中文译题

一列快速列车驶过长度为 $210\,\mathrm{m}$ 的车站，车头进入车站时的速率为 $22.0\,\mathrm{m/s}$，随后以 $0.150\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$ 的减速度运行。（a）车头在站内停留多长时间？（b）车头离站时的速率多大？（c）若列车全长为 $130\,\mathrm{m}$，车尾何时离开车站？（d）车尾离站时的速度是多少？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Motion Equations for Constant Acceleration in One Dimension — CP-00188

An express train passes through a station. It enters with an initial velocity of 22.0 m/s and decelerates at a rate of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mtext>.</mtext><msup><mtext>150 m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{0 "." "150 m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 as it goes through. The station is 210 m long. (a) How long is the nose of the train in the station? (b) How fast is it going when the nose leaves the station? (c) If the train is 130 m long, when does the end of the train leave the station? (d) What is the velocity of the end of the train as it leaves?

## CP-00189｜自行车运动员冲刺后领先多少

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 一维匀加速运动／自行车冲刺

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一名自行车运动员在比赛末段开始冲刺，初速 $11.5\,\mathrm{m/s}$，在 $7.00\,\mathrm{s}$ 内以 $0.500\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$ 的加速度前进。（a）求冲刺后的速度；（b）此后以该速度骑到终点，若开始冲刺时距终点 $300\,\mathrm{m}$，求他相对于一直以原速骑行的情形节省了多少时间；（c）另一名选手在他开始冲刺时领先 $5.00\,\mathrm{m}$，但不能加速，以 $11.8\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 匀速骑到终点。最终获胜者在距离和时间上领先多少？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Motion Equations for Constant Acceleration in One Dimension — CP-00189

A bicycle racer sprints at the end of a race to clinch a victory. The racer has an initial velocity of 11.5 m/s and accelerates at the rate of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn><mtext>.</mtext><msup><mtext>500 m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{0 "." "500 m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

 for 7.00 s. (a) What is his final velocity? (b) The racer continues at this velocity to the finish line. If he was 300 m from the finish line when he started to accelerate, how much time did he save? (c) One other racer was 5.00 m ahead when the winner started to accelerate, but he was unable to accelerate, and traveled at 11.8 m/s until the finish line. How far ahead of him (in meters and in seconds) did the winner finish?

## CP-00190｜摩托车在盐滩赛道的行驶时间

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 一维匀加速运动／分段运动

## 中文译题

原题讲述：1967 年，新西兰车手 Burt Munro 在美国犹他州邦纳维尔盐滩驾驶 Indian 摩托车创下纪录，最高速率为 $183.58\,\mathrm{mi/h}$。单向赛道长 $5.00\,\mathrm{mi}$。若摩托车从静止加速到 $60.0\,\mathrm{mi/h}$ 需 $4.00\,\mathrm{s}$，并保持同一加速度直至达到最高速率，求完成整条赛道的时间。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Motion Equations for Constant Acceleration in One Dimension — CP-00190

In 1967, New Zealander Burt Munro set the world record for an Indian motorcycle, on the Bonneville Salt Flats in Utah, with a maximum speed of 183.58 mi/h. The one-way course was 5.00 mi long. Acceleration rates are often described by the time it takes to reach 60.0 mi/h from rest. If this time was 4.00 s, and Burt accelerated at this rate until he reached his maximum speed, how long did it take Burt to complete the course?

## CP-00191｜博尔特百米与二百米的最大速率

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 一维匀加速运动／短跑分段模型

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

（a）2008 年北京奥运会男子 $100\,\mathrm{m}$ 决赛中，牙买加运动员尤塞恩·博尔特以 $9.69\,\mathrm{s}$ 的成绩打破世界纪录。假设他前 $3.00\,\mathrm{s}$ 匀加速至最大速率，随后保持这一速率跑到终点，求其最大速率与加速度。（b）在同届奥运会的 $200\,\mathrm{m}$ 比赛中，他又以 $19.30\,\mathrm{s}$ 的成绩打破世界纪录；作同样假设，求该项目中的最大速率。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Motion Equations for Constant Acceleration in One Dimension — CP-00191

(a) A world record was set for the men’s 100-m dash in the 2008 Olympic Games in Beijing by Usain Bolt of Jamaica. Bolt “coasted” across the finish line with a time of 9.69 s. If we assume that Bolt accelerated for 3.00 s to reach his maximum speed, and maintained that speed for the rest of the race, calculate his maximum speed and his acceleration. (b) During the same Olympics, Bolt also set the world record in the 200-m dash with a time of 19.30 s. Using the same assumptions as for the 100-m dash, what was his maximum speed for this race?

<a id="cp-00198"></a>

## CP-00198｜John Paul Stapp 火箭滑车实验的加减速度

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 运动学／火箭滑车的极端加减速

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

**职业应用题。**美国空军军官 John Paul Stapp 研究极端减速对人体的影响。1954 年 12 月 10 日，他乘坐火箭滑车，由静止在 $5.00\,\mathrm{s}$ 内加速至 $282\,\mathrm{m/s}$（$1015\,\mathrm{km/h}$），随后在仅 $1.40\,\mathrm{s}$ 内减速至停止。分别求（a）加速时的加速度，（b）制动时的减速度，并以 $g=9.80\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$ 的倍数表示。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Acceleration — CP-00198

**Professional Application**

Dr. John Paul Stapp was U.S. Air Force officer who studied the effects of extreme deceleration on the human body. On December 10, 1954, Stapp rode a rocket sled, accelerating from rest to a top speed of 282 m/s (1015 km/h) in 5.00 s, and was brought jarringly back to rest in only 1.40 s! Calculate his (a) acceleration and (b) deceleration. Express each in multiples of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow></semantics></math>

 <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>9</mn><mtext>.</mtext><mtext>80 m</mtext><msup><mtext>/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow></semantics></math>

 by taking its ratio to the acceleration of gravity.

一名通勤者倒车驶出车库，加速度大小为 $1.40\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$。（a）由静止达到 $2.00\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 的速率需要多久？（b）之后她在 $0.800\,\mathrm{s}$ 内制动至停止，减速度大小为多少？

A commuter backs her car out of her garage with an acceleration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mtext>.</mtext><msup><mtext>40 m/s</mtext><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mrow /></mrow><annotation encoding="StarMath 5.0"> size 12{1 "." "40 m/s" rSup { size 8{2} } } {}</annotation></semantics></math>

. (a) How long does it take her to reach a speed of 2.00 m/s? (b) If she then brakes to a stop in 0.800 s, what is her deceleration?

假设一枚洲际弹道导弹在 $60.0\,\mathrm{s}$ 内从静止达到 $6.50\,\mathrm{km/s}$ 的亚轨道速率（原题注明实际速度与时间数据保密）。求平均加速度，分别用 $\mathrm{m/s^2}$ 和 $g=9.80\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$ 的倍数表示。

 and in multiples of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow /></mrow></semantics></math>

## CP-00203｜石块上升和下降时撞击椰子的速度

## 中文译题

假设你朝棕榈树上的椰子几乎竖直向上扔出一块石头，石头在上升过程中没有击中椰子，却在下降时击中它。忽略空气阻力，石头下降击中椰子时的速率与它若在上升过程中于同高度击中椰子时的速率相比如何？它在上升还是下降时更容易使椰子脱落？解释。

Is it more likely to dislodge the coconut on the way up or down? Explain.

<a id="cp-00210"></a>

## CP-00210｜救援直升机投下救生圈的高度

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 自由落体／竖直下抛与高度

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一架救援直升机悬停在一名沉船者上方，救援人员以 $1.40\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 的初速度竖直向下抛出救生圈，观察到救生圈在 $1.8\,\mathrm{s}$ 后到达水面。（a）列出已知量；（b）求救生圈释放点高于水面的高度。原题说明直升机向下吹出的气流使空气阻力的影响较小，因此可近似采用重力加速度。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Falling Objects — CP-00210

A rescue helicopter is hovering over a person whose boat has sunk. One of the rescuers throws a life preserver straight down to the victim with an initial velocity of 1.40 m/s and observes that it takes 1.8 s to reach the water. (a) List the knowns in this problem. (b) How high above the water was the preserver released? Note that the downdraft of the helicopter reduces the effects of air resistance on the falling life preserver, so that an acceleration equal to that of gravity is reasonable.

## CP-00211｜海豚跃出水面的最高高度和滞空时间

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 自由落体／海豚竖直跳跃

## 中文译题

一只海豚在水上表演中以 $13.0\,\mathrm{m/s}$ 的竖直向上初速度跃出水面。（a）列出已知量；（b）求海豚身体上升到的最高水面以上高度；先确定最高点速度作为已知量、列出未知量，解释选用的方程并展示步骤、检查单位及答案合理性；（c）求海豚在空中停留的总时间。忽略体型及姿态影响。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Falling Objects — CP-00211

A dolphin in an aquatic show jumps straight up out of the water at a velocity of 13.0 m/s. (a) List the knowns in this problem. (b) How high does his body rise above the water? To solve this part, first note that the final velocity is now a known and identify its value. Then identify the unknown, and discuss how you chose the appropriate equation to solve for it. Neglect any effects due to his size or orientation.

## CP-00219｜优胜美地落石的下落与预警时间

## 中文译题

美国优胜美地国家公园半圆丘附近有一处高约 $250\,\mathrm{m}$ 的悬崖。假设一块岩石从崖顶松脱。（a）求岩石落地时的速率；（b）若山下游客的反应时间为 $0.300\,\mathrm{s}$，他在听到岩石松动声后还剩多长时间可以避让？忽略游客自身高度。当天声速为 $335\,\mathrm{m/s}$。

(b) Assuming a reaction time of 0.300 s, how long will a tourist at the bottom have to get out of the way after hearing the sound of the rock breaking loose (neglecting the height of the tourist, which would become negligible anyway if hit)? The speed of sound is 335 m/s on this day.

<a id="cp-00220"></a>

## CP-00220｜上抛球通过窗户所需时间反推初速度

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 自由落体／穿越窗口的速度

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一个球竖直向上抛出，在上升过程中经过一扇高 $2.00\,\mathrm{m}$、窗底距地面 $7.50\,\mathrm{m}$ 的窗户。球从窗底到窗顶历时 $0.312\,\mathrm{s}$。求球的初速度。提示：先利用窗户高度与通过时间求球经过窗底时的速度，再用出手点至窗底的距离，反推发射初速度。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Falling Objects — CP-00220

A ball is thrown straight up. It passes a 2.00-m-high window 7.50 m off the ground on its path up and takes 0.312 s to go past the window. What was the ball’s initial velocity? Hint: First consider only the distance along the window, and solve for the ball's velocity at the bottom of the window. Next, consider only the distance from the ground to the bottom of the window, and solve for the initial velocity using the velocity at the bottom of the window as the final velocity.

. (d) How much did the ball compress during its collision with the floor, assuming the floor is absolutely rigid?

<a id="cp-00262"></a>

## CP-00262｜两步位移后返回原点的条件

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 位移矢量／合位移

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

假设先后走了两段均非零的位移 $\mathbf A$、$\mathbf B$。（a）什么情况下能回到出发点？（b）更一般地，两个非零矢量的和何时为零？（c）最终离起点的最大距离是否为两段位移长度之和？

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Vector Addition and Subtraction: Graphical Methods — CP-00262

Suppose you take two steps <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="bold">A</mtext></mrow></mrow></semantics></math>

 and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="bold">B</mtext></mrow></mrow></semantics></math>

 (that is, two nonzero displacements). Under what circumstances can you end up at your starting point? More generally, under what circumstances can two nonzero vectors add to give zero? Is the maximum distance you can end up from the starting point <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mtext mathvariant="bold">A</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext mathvariant="bold">B</mtext></mrow></mrow></semantics></math>

 the sum of the lengths of the two steps?

<a id="cp-00300"></a>

## CP-00300｜旋转圆筒中的乘客

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 非惯性参考系／向心力与静摩擦

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

某游乐设施是一个巨大的竖直圆筒。乘客进入圆筒，站在其水平地板上并靠着筒壁；随后圆筒逐渐加速旋转，地板向下移开。乘客感觉自己仿佛被某种类似重力的力压在筒壁上。这是在圆筒的旋转参考系中用来解释现象的惯性力（虚拟力）。请在近似为惯性参考系的地面参考系中，解释是什么使乘客保持贴靠筒壁，并指出作用在乘客身上的全部真实力。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Fictitious Forces and Non-inertial Frames: The Coriolis Force — CP-00300

In one amusement park ride, riders enter a large vertical barrel and stand against the wall on its horizontal floor. The barrel is spun up and the floor drops away. Riders feel as if they are pinned to the wall by a force something like the gravitational force. This is a fictitious force sensed and used by the riders to explain events in the rotating frame of reference of the barrel. Explain in an inertial frame of reference (Earth is nearly one) what pins the riders to the wall, and identify all of the real forces acting on them.

## CP-00400｜缓冲材料如何减小碰撞力

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 动量定理／冲量

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

从冲量的角度解释，为什么缓冲垫能够减小碰撞时的作用力。请结合实际例子说明，例如比较托育场所使用地毯地面与瓷砖地面各有什么差别。

# Impulse — CP-00400

<strong>Professional Application </strong>

****Explain in terms of impulse how padding reduces forces in a collision. State this in terms of a real example, such as the advantages of a carpeted vs. tile floor for a day care center.

<a id="ip-0004"></a>

## IP-0004｜绕地球极轨运行卫星的位移

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 运动学／位移矢量

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

一颗卫星在距地表 $400\,\mathrm{km}$ 的高度沿圆形极轨道绕地球运行，即轨道经过北极和南极上空。卫星由正位于北极上空的位置运动到南纬 $45^\circ$ 上空时，求其**位移矢量的大小和方向**。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Polar Orbiting Satellite

A satellite is in a circular polar orbit around Earth at an altitude of $400\ {\rm km}$, meaning it passes directly over the North and South Poles. What are the magnitude and direction of the displacement vector from when the satellite is directly over the North Pole to when it is at latitude $-45^\circ$?

<a id="ip-0005"></a>

## IP-0005｜从位置矢量求瞬时速度和平均速度

**学科：** 力学　**知识点：** 运动学／三维速度与平均速度

**审核状态：** 中文初译；待独立双语、物理、答案及版权复核；不可正式组卷。

## 中文译题

粒子的位置随时间变化为
$$\vec r(t)=(2.0t^2)\,\hat i+(2.0+3.0t)\,\hat j+5.0t\,\hat k\quad(\mathrm{km}).$$
（a）求 $t=2.0\,\mathrm{s}$ 时的**瞬时速度矢量**与**速率**；（b）求 $t=1.0\,\mathrm{s}$ 至 $t=3.0\,\mathrm{s}$ 间的**平均速度矢量**。

## 英文原题（存档摘录）

# Calculating the Velocity Vector

The position function of a particle is  
$\vec{r}(t) = (2.0t^2)\ \hat{i} + (2.0 + 3.0t)\ \hat{j} + 5.0t\ \hat{k}\ {\rm km}.$  (a) What is the instantaneous velocity vector and speed at $t = 2.0\ {\rm s}$? (b) What is the average velocity between $t = 1.0\ {\rm s}$ and $t = 3.0\ {\rm s}$?

## 来源

- 原件：external_sources/intro_physics/raw/IP-0005.md
